crates/lp-simplex/src/phase1.rs Two-phase Phase I: auxiliary objective; primal loop; residual check; artificial removal; return feasible Basis or Status::Infeasible.
crates/lp-simplex/src/phase2.rs Phase II loop: entering (lp-pricing) → ratio test → pivot → optimality check; unboundedness detection emitting PrimalRay.
crates/lp-simplex/src/driver.rs SimplexSolver::solve: orchestrate presolve → Phase I → Phase II → dual simplex (warm) → sensitivity; select dense vs revised path by problem size.
crates/lp-simplex/tests/small_lp.rs End-to-end tests on hand-crafted LPs; known optima; infeasible and unbounded cases.
crates/lp-pricing/Cargo.toml Depends on lp-core; pure pivot-selection logic.
crates/lp-pricing/src/lib.rs Re-exports PricingStrategy, select_entering, select_leaving.
crates/lp-pricing/src/dantzig.rs Dantzig rule: argmin r_j over candidates; returns Option<usize>.
crates/lp-pricing/src/bland.rs Bland's rule: smallest-index negative reduced cost; smallest-index leaving tie-break; I7 guarantee.
crates/lp-pricing/src/devex.rs Devex: weight initialization (1.0), per-pivot update, entering score r_j²/w_j; reset on refactorization.
crates/lp-pricing/tests/anti_cycling.rs Beale (1955) and Kuhn cycling instances; finite termination with Bland; correct optimum.
crates/lp-revised/Cargo.toml Depends on lp-core, lp-model, lp-lu, lp-pricing.
crates/lp-revised/src/lib.rs Re-exports RevisedSimplex, Basis.
crates/lp-revised/src/revised.rs Revised loop: BTRAN for prices, reduced costs, entering (pricing), FTRAN for column, ratio test, basis update.
crates/lp-revised/src/basis.rs Basis management: basic[], status[]; FTRAN/BTRAN delegation to lp-lu; basic_solution via triangular solve.
crates/lp-revised/tests/vs_tableau.rs Differential tests: revised vs dense on small LP suite; objectives match within 1e-10.
crates/lp-lu/Cargo.toml Depends on lp-core; pure numerical crate; no LP semantics.
crates/lp-lu/src/lib.rs Re-exports LuFactorization, factorize, ftran, btran, update, needs_refactor.
crates/lp-lu/src/factor.rs Sparse LU with Markowitz partial pivoting; produces l_eta, u, row_perm, col_perm; near-singular detection via PIVOT_TOL.
crates/lp-lu/src/ftran.rs Forward solve B d = a: L factors (eta sequence) then U; returns sparse d.
crates/lp-lu/src/btran.rs Backward solve B^T y = c_B: reverse eta sequence, U^T then L^T; returns dual prices y.
crates/lp-lu/src/update.rs Forrest-Tomlin rank-1 update: modify U, append eta, increment n_updates, set refactor flag.
crates/lp-lu/src/refactor.rs Refactorization trigger: check n_updates > REFACTOR_LIMIT or fill ratio; call factorize; reset n_updates.
crates/lp-lu/tests/lu_correctness.rs Verify L*U == P*A*Q; FTRAN/BTRAN round-trip residual < 1e-12; update-then-refactor consistency.
crates/lp-bounds/Cargo.toml Depends on lp-core, lp-model.
crates/lp-bounds/src/lib.rs Re-exports BoundedRatioTest, apply_bound_flip.
crates/lp-bounds/src/ratio.rs Bounded ratio test: step vs x_B_i - lb_i and ub_i - x_B_i; entering variable range check; RatioTestResult.
crates/lp-bounds/src/flip.rs Bound flip: move entering nonbasic to opposite bound; update objective delta; update VarStatus; no basis change.
crates/lp-bounds/tests/bounded.rs LPs with finite upper bounds; bound-flip detection; lb <= x <= ub in solution; size vs slack-row formulation.
crates/lp-dual/Cargo.toml Depends on lp-core, lp-model, lp-revised, lp-lu.
crates/lp-dual/src/lib.rs Re-exports DualSimplex, warm_start.
crates/lp-dual/src/dual.rs Dual loop: select leaving (most infeasible basic), BTRAN dual row, dual ratio test for entering, pivot.
crates/lp-dual/src/warm.rs Warm start: accept Basis; verify dual feasibility; enter dual loop; return Solution.
crates/lp-dual/tests/reopt.rs Warm-start reoptimization: solve → perturb RHS → reoptimize; objective match within 1e-8; fewer pivots than cold.
crates/lp-presolve/Cargo.toml Depends on lp-core, lp-model.
crates/lp-presolve/src/lib.rs Re-exports Presolve, Scale, postsolve.
crates/lp-presolve/src/presolve.rs Presolve: empty row/col removal → singleton substitution → redundancy detection → bound tightening → fixed-variable elimination; operation stack.
crates/lp-presolve/src/scale.rs Geometric scaling + Curtis-Reid iterative scaling; compute/apply/store row-col scale vectors.
crates/lp-presolve/src/postsolve.rs Reverse presolve stack; unscale; map reduced solution to original space; verify I9.
crates/lp-presolve/tests/presolve_tests.rs Each reduction rule tested independently; presolved optimal = original optimal on 10 LPs; postsolve feasibility.
crates/lp-sensitivity/Cargo.toml Depends on lp-core, lp-model, lp-revised.
crates/lp-sensitivity/src/lib.rs Re-exports compute_sensitivity, Sensitivity.
crates/lp-sensitivity/src/ranging.rs Cost-coefficient ranging (delta keeping dual feasibility) and RHS ranging (delta keeping primal feasibility).
crates/lp-sensitivity/src/shadow.rs Extract shadow prices from Solution.duals_y; reduced-cost ranges for nonbasic variables.
crates/lp-sensitivity/tests/sensitivity_tests.rs Verify ranges against analytic results for textbook LPs; shadow price sign convention for Le/Ge.
crates/lp-parse/Cargo.toml Depends on lp-core, lp-model; no solver side-effects.
crates/lp-parse/src/lib.rs Re-exports MpsParser, LpFileParser, SolutionWriter.
crates/lp-parse/src/mps.rs MPS fixed + free parser: NAME/ROWS/COLUMNS/RHS/BOUNDS/RANGES/OBJSENSE; emits LpProblem; errors on unknown section.
crates/lp-parse/src/lp_file.rs LP file parser (CPLEX/GLPK style): minimize/maximize, subject to, bounds, general, binary; inline comments.
crates/lp-parse/src/writer.rs Write Solution to human-readable text and optional MPS SOL format.
crates/lp-parse/tests/parse_roundtrip.rs Parse MPS → write LP → re-parse; compare objective, variable count, constraint count; afiro.mps round-trip.
crates/lp-cli/Cargo.toml Binary crate; depends on all solver crates plus clap (derive feature).
crates/lp-cli/src/main.rs Entry point; clap top-level CLI with subcommands: solve, sensitivity, convert, check.
crates/lp-cli/src/commands/solve.rs solve: parse → presolve → solve → postsolve → print solution, status, certificate type.
crates/lp-cli/src/commands/sensitivity.rs sensitivity: solve then compute Sensitivity; print ranging table (cost, RHS, shadow prices).
crates/lp-cli/src/commands/convert.rs convert: read MPS → write LP file (or vice versa); validate on parse; report errors.
crates/lp-cli/src/commands/check.rs check: read LP + solution file; verify all constraints within FEASIBILITY_TOL; report per-constraint pass/fail.
tests/conformance/netlib_runner.rs Iterate Netlib LP set; solve each; compare objective to reference within 1e-6; report pass rate.
tests/conformance/netlib_expected.csv Reference optima: columns name, sense, optimal_value, status for each Netlib instance.
tests/conformance/kkt_verifier.rs For every Status::Optimal: check primal feasibility, dual feasibility, complementary slackness; all within tolerances.
tests/integration/cycling.rs Beale (1955) and Kuhn instances; finite termination with Bland; correct optimum.
tests/integration/infeasible.rs Infeasible instances; Status::Infeasible; Certificate::DualRay satisfies Farkas conditions.
tests/integration/unbounded.rs Unbounded instances; Status::Unbounded; Certificate::PrimalRay valid direction and feasibility.
tests/integration/bounds.rs LPs with general bounds; lb <= x <= ub in solution; objective matches reference.
benches/simplex_bench.rs criterion benchmarks: afiro, adlittle, sc205; wall time and pivot count; regression tracking.
benches/lu_bench.rs LU factorization and FTRAN/BTRAN micro-benchmarks on random sparse bases; sizes 50, 200, 1000.

Appendix B — Milestone Summary

Nominal total: 18 weeks for Phases 1–14. Phase-0-complete is the blueprint-authored state (no code).

Phase Deliverable Duration Key Risks Exit Criteria
1 lp-core types; lp-model LpProblem + CSC SparseMatrix + ProblemBuilder; validation 1 week Incorrect Bound semantics (−∞/+∞); dimension mismatch silently accepted; Maximize/Minimize sign confusion Unit tests pass; builder round-trips 10-var LP; dimension checks reject malformed input; I10 setup
2 Standard-form conversion: slack/surplus/artificial; free-variable handling; StandardForm 1 week Free-variable split causing index aliasing; artificial count off by one; normalization sign error StandardForm column count matches n_struct + n_slack + n_artificial; I10 verified on known instances
3 Dense Tableau; pivot operation; reduced-cost extraction; reference engine 1 week Off-by-one in pivot column indexing; objective row sign convention; basis column set desync 2×2 and 3×3 pivot tests pass; identity-basis round-trips; reduced costs match hand computation
4 Primal Phase II (Dantzig + dense path); unboundedness detection; Certificate::PrimalRay 1.5 weeks Infinite loop before anti-cycling added; ratio-test sign error; wrong ray direction 5 textbook LPs reach known optima; unbounded flag with valid ray on 2 unbounded instances; I6 checked
5 Two-phase Phase I; feasibility check; artificial removal; Status::Infeasible + Certificate::DualRay 1.5 weeks Phase I optimum falsely declared zero; artificials at zero causing spurious infeasibility; wrong dual ray Infeasible instances rejected with valid dual ray; 3 nontrivial feasible LPs reach Phase II with correct BFS
6 Anti-cycling: Bland + Devex + stall detection + rule-switching in lp-pricing 1 week Bland degrades non-degenerate performance; stall detector false-positives; Devex weights diverge Beale and Kuhn tests terminate finitely; PricingStrategy switches correctly; I7 verified
7 Degenerate-pivot handling; Certificate emission for all three Status variants; KKT stub 1 week Certificate built from wrong dual values; PrimalRay not feasible direction; DualRay fails Farkas check kkt_verifier.rs passes on all Optimal results; ray certificates pass direction and feasibility checks
8 Bounded-variable simplex: VarStatus::NonbasicUpper; bound flip; bounded ratio test 1 week Ratio test misses bound-flip case; solution violates upper bounds; VarStatus inconsistent with basis Bounded LP suite passes; lb <= x <= ub within tolerance; I6 extended to bound-flip case
9 Revised simplex (FTRAN/BTRAN via lp-lu); same output as dense path 2 weeks Numerical drift accumulating over many pivots; reduced-cost discrepancy; basis sync errors vs_tableau.rs differential tests pass; objectives match within 1e-10; P4 production path active
10 Sparse LU + Forrest-Tomlin updates + Markowitz ordering + refactorization 2 weeks Fill growth breaking sparsity; update instability after many eta vectors; Markowitz overhead lu_correctness.rs passes; FTRAN/BTRAN residual < 1e-12; refactorization triggered correctly; I1 verified
11 Dual simplex; warm-start API; dual ratio test; I3 maintenance 1.5 weeks Saved basis not dual-feasible after perturbation; dual ratio selects wrong entering; unbounded dual reopt.rs objective within 1e-8 of cold; warm start uses fewer pivots; I3 verified throughout loop
12 Presolve + scaling; postsolve; lp-sensitivity cost/RHS ranging + shadow prices 2 weeks Postsolve incorrect on fixed variables; scaling breaks LU conditioning; sensitivity bounds wrong direction presolve_tests.rs passes; presolved = original optimal; sensitivity verified on textbook examples; I9 checked
13 lp-parse MPS + LP readers; lp-cli four subcommands; end-to-end pipeline 1 week MPS free-format edge cases; CLI wiring errors; presolve–postsolve integration bugs CLI solves afiro.mps correctly; convert round-trip parses; check rejects infeasible solutions
14 Conformance (Netlib); KKT verification; cycling stress; benchmarks; docs 1 week Hidden numerical failures on Netlib; benchmark regression; documentation gaps All Netlib within 1e-6; kkt_verifier.rs 100%; Beale/Kuhn terminate; bench baselines recorded; docs complete
